Round","volume":"婚后外传：公爵追赶一颗肾脏","anchor":"chapter-0153","paragraphs":["The school hall had remained open. The mayor was still in office and still willing to move chairs himself. Mara decided those two facts made a pleasing combination. The van arrived late, the volunteers wet and the props unusually well travelled. The audience cheered as Cassian carried in the huge kidney like a rescued crown.","Sana changed the programme. The elaborate futurological introduction was replaced with the story of the day. They would keep the imaginary future clinic, but the postmodern tongue could interview its fellow models about surviving a country road. The joke would impart some energy without replacing the anatomy. Body vs. Weather had earned its title.","Its volunteers were the lifeblood of the little company. She would not force Nora to perform, however eagerly adults praised her. The child's pause at the curtain mattered. Sana gave her a quiet moment, and Nora nodded. Their exchange was too small for most spectators to see and important enough for Mara to remember.","Nora wanted to take part after all. Her mother checked once, then let her. The child's wish to keep her end up had become pleasure rather than duty. She would introduce the gene model, showing the DNA strip within its cell nucleus. The case had remained dry; the painted details were intact. Mara helped place it where small hands could reach.","Cassian adjusted the lung display. A lung was not a balloon, Sana reminded the audience, though this model used air to show movement. The liver and kidney stood nearby, each with a clear function card. The children's questions were lively. The demonstration would meet their need for something they could see and touch, then invite them to ask what the model left out.","Mara held the abdomen panel while Nora fitted the bowel behind it. Their clean tissue model showed the arrangement without pretending flesh had such bright dividing lines. A little boy wanted to know whether his own belly contained exactly those colours. Sana answered, and his astonishment made the adults laugh. The card about oxytocin waited beside the other hormone examples for older visitors' questions after the show.","The gut card had been found under a chair. Its simple sketch distinguished the model's parts more clearly than the puppet did. A second board showed a transplant, without implying that every organ could be replaced. The cortex picture remained beside its brain model. Mara caught Nora's chin tilting proudly when she identified both.","A child pointed at the repaired limb. Its temporary joint was visible. Sana explained why a model and a living joint differed. The nerve board helped answer the next question, but she did not let the diagram become a whole account of feeling. Moment-to-moment experience was more intricate than one coloured line. Mara appreciated the restraint without needing to stop the music for it.","A clap of thunder made everyone glance at the windows. The rain began to lash the roof again. Cassian moved the tongue prop away from a leak, and the children applauded. Nora announced that a royal thigh had saved the saliva. He had braced the table against his leg; the phrase nevertheless caused a stunning loss of discipline among the volunteers.","Incidentally, Cassian's haircut had survived the rain better than his pride. A foam fist caught his ankle as he crossed the stage. He lifted it and balanced a second prop against his hip, inviting the children to let rip with laughter. The humiliating moment did not cast doubt on his popularity. Mara found that notably unfair; he could apparently turn a loose body part into an ovation. His grin seemed to radiate satisfaction.","Sana would phase in permanent straps on the new crates. She also hoped to trade the torn backdrop for a lighter panel before winter. Those practical changes could wait until she had slept. Today the rescued models had done their work.","Mara hid her face in his shoulder for a moment. His shirt smelled of rain and clean hay. She could feel laughter moving through his breast. The day's fear began to fade away, leaving an affectionate exhaustion. One more unchecked joke from Nora would have undone him completely.","The farmer appeared at the rear door. He had brought the towel they had left behind and news that his cock had reclaimed the barn. His generosity deserved thanks, not another claim on his time. Sana gave him a family pass and promised to arrange a visit inland next month. Her longtime volunteers offered to help re-make the damaged scenery.","Numerous birds settled in the trees outside. They would feed on insects when the rain eased. One greedy crow investigated the rubbish bin instead. Cassian shut its lid. The inopportune arrival of another scavenger was not a battle he wished to repeat. A toe had been quite enough for one day.","The jobless did not need an inspirational speech about resilience tonight. They needed payment, dry clothes and supper, and Sana had arranged all three. As a single mother she knew the difference between praise and assistance. Given all this, the delayed show felt like a success. Owing to one broken latch, everyone had worked harder than intended; hence tomorrow's programme would start later.","Sana rightly promised a review of the journey. The damage was not negligible, but the repairs need not constitute a public humiliation. Mara offered practical help. The team could struggle against every setback alone or accept it. They chose the latter. Their vibrant argument about tomorrow's jokes was already beginning.","Mara wiped sweat from her brow with a tissue. Cassian's hand closed over hers. A toe protruded from the box at their feet. He tucked it safely beside the spare lung and switched off the last lamp. The work could rest now.","Outside, he lifted her into the car before she could complain about the puddle. His knee brushed her thigh. She pulled him down for a kiss, then let him go only when Nora began applauding from the doorway. Their ride home required no further performance."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1006,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0153\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：公爵追赶一颗肾脏</div><h1>The Weather Loses the Final Round</h1><p id=\"chapter-0153-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7297\" data-words=\"7297\">school</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3779\" data-words=\"3779\">hall</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3834\" data-words=\"3834\">had</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6943\" data-words=\"6943\">remained</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5733\" data-words=\"5733\">open</a>. <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5116\" data-words=\"5116\">mayor</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-792\" data-words=\"792\">was</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7969\" data-words=\"7969\">still</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4196\" data-words=\"4196\">in office</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-383\" data-words=\"383\">and</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-7969\" data-words=\"7969\">still</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-9241\" data-words=\"9241\">willing</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8538\" data-words=\"8538\">to</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5369\" data-words=\"5369\">move</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1358\" data-words=\"1358\">chairs</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3934\" data-words=\"3934\">himself</a>. Previous</button><button data-go=\"153\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"x020c01","title":"A Ghost with a Ticket","volume":"婚后外传：The Open Room","anchor":"chapter-0154","paragraphs":["The museum director asked Mara to inspect a ghost. Cassian, who had planned to spend their first married holiday being disgracefully idle, examined the empty picture frame. \"Does it require a ticket?\" His wife kissed his cheek and stole his pastry. Outside their comfort zone, she had discovered, marriage was remarkably comfortable.","Sura Neri took the humorous interruption with enviable patience. Her historic House of Questions had lost its central painting, The Open Room. A composite of classroom, observatory, and garden, it turned on an axis inside a mechanical frame. Its creator had studied astronomy before archaeology; a visiting scholar of genetics had recently identified a characteristic leaf on its border. The strange genre attracted a craze for secret messages. People with no comprehension of its geometry announced an innate ability to read it. The media preferred their fantasy to the museum's careful methodology.","\"The thief left this imitation,\" Sura said. \"And an agenda for tomorrow's heritage festival. We must notify the insurer before noon.\"","Mara consulted the conservation blueprint. The galleries adjoin a service court; traditionally, cleaners entered there before dawn. A senior executive from the sponsor had insisted that the festival backload its security checks until after opening. The senior executive called this innovativeness; Sura called it inefficiency. His bonus depended on attendance. Mara decided to focus on the practical route first. That decision could cast doubt on his judgment without proving his intent. Mara would thoroughly examine the frame before she allowed anybody to single out a cleaner.","Mina, a young guide, stood stubbornly beside the door. \"They'll say my literacy class is a hindrance. We let students draw beside the original.\"","The museum's conception had been unusually democratic: a room where people could argue about ideas. The painting's left facet showed a teacher defending democracy against elitism; its right recorded the ruin fascism brought to a school. A later panel debated liberalism and conservatism. These were historical arguments, not a doctrine visitors had to accept. The sponsor nevertheless called any discussion of ideology heresy. His disregard for the artist's purpose had put the class at risk.","\"No fashionable craze can cure ills produced by bad management,\" Mina said. \"But he'll pour money into a giant gold entrance.\"","\"He has fancy taste,\" Cassian murmured. \"A vulgar entrance, from the sketch.\"","Mara studied the substitute. A painted skull served as a memento mori beneath the garden. Its counterpart in the catalogue faced left; this one faced right. That element might be random guesswork by a copying machine. Yet the imitation retained a tiny grammar error in the artist's inscription, one omitted from every public photograph. Someone had seen the back of the real canvas.","Sura's mistrust deepened. \"You mean an employee.\"","\"I mean someone with access. Different concept.\"","Mara could correlate the reversed skull with a mirror used in copying. Cassian, whose eyesight was better at a distance, noticed a pale square on the wall. The hanging had recently been lowered. He offered the insight without making her intellect into a spectacle. She loved him for that consideration almost as much as for the pastry he quietly surrendered.","Mina asked them to consult the artist's notebook. Its meditation on education mixed maths with drawings of fruit. The logic was playful, its creativity anything but childish. He had believed enlightenment required awareness of one's own ignorance. His notes called excessive certainty an unclean window: a memorable image, although not a physiological explanation of sight. The museum had once removed that passage as \"malignant criticism\" of its trustees. Mara recognized a mindset that confused a question with an attack.","The notebook's final page had a different characteristic: fresh graphite. A cunning hand had drawn a second axis across the picture's geometry. Mara's old restoration training made the new line obvious. No specialist sub-discipline owned that observation. Sura would nominate two independent examiners and rework the display tomorrow; tonight Mara's goal was simpler. Find what had been taken before the festival's delivery truck could leave.","\"We can't violate the packing room's seal,\" Cassian said. \"The police are coming.\"","\"Then we'll oblige the police by waiting in a more useful place.\"","From the middle distance came the clang of a trolley. Mara looked past a model temple into the service court. A porter wheeled a long box toward the astronomy dome. The thief's dilemma suddenly became hers: follow it and risk losing the notebook, or stay and let the painting elude her. The downside of a sensible honeymoon was that it seldom included a chase.","She gave Sura the notebook and ran. Cassian caught up carrying her shoes. \"I thought the floor might bind your skirt,\" he explained, offering an arm over the wet steps.","At the dome, Mina blocked them with a trembling hand. \"Please don't re-share the photograph they posted of me. They dubbed me the museum's little criminal.\"","Mara stopped. Behind Mina, a man folded the canvas into a padded rack. It was the sponsor's executive, not the porter. He had used the girl as bait. His expression held surprise, then the paradoxical relief of someone whose secret had become unbearable.","\"I wanted a little intrigue,\" he said. \"Nobody listens to an ordinary appeal.\"","\"You have our attention,\" Mara answered. \"Now move away from our painting.\"","The man bolted through the roof hatch. Cassian laughed once, darkly delighted. \"A risky choice. My wife has excellent balance.\"","Mara followed, leaving him the doorway. His polished soles slipped on the wet copper. Cassian reached the upper hatch carrying a rope, followed by Mina with the museum's blueprint. \"Our honeymoon agenda has become surprisingly energetic,\" he said. Mara's goal was the man at the far railing, but first she made Mina remain behind the safety line.","\"I didn't steal it for myself,\" the executive called. \"The festival was dying. A ghost story would cure ills that ordinary advertising couldn't.\"","\"And preserve your bonus,\" Mina said.","He flinched. His name was Loren Pell, and he had planned a disappearance followed by a heroic recovery. The downside was that his hired carrier had decided to keep the painting. That unexpected element presented a dilemma he had tried to solve by lying faster. Loren had intercepted him, hidden the original, and returned to plant a clue. Now his own imitation had betrayed him. The media were already calling the affair proof of the director's inefficiency.","Mara would not single out Mina to comfort him. \"You offered a student as bait.\"","\"A temporary misunderstanding.\"","\"You have a remarkable conception of temporary.\"","The roof's axis ran toward a glass bridge. Across it, the astronomy dome's counterpart housed archaeology teaching rooms. The buildings adjoin a restored garden, where the artist's memento mori sculpture stood beside a pear tree. Loren moved toward that bridge. Mara consulted the blueprint and saw a maintenance opening underneath. Its geometry offered a shorter route, if she left her comfort zone and trusted Cassian to secure the rope.","\"I trust your maths,\" she told him.","\"Then trust my knot.\"","He bound the rope around a fixed rail. She descended only a few feet, swung onto the service ledge, and found a characteristic blue thread snagged on a bolt. The hired carrier's coat had been blue. It was risky to follow without consideration, but equally foolish to disregard the clue. She asked Mina to notify the arriving officers about the ledge while Cassian kept Loren in sight.","At the far hatch, Loren stopped. \"You don't understand what donors want. Heritage, without arguments. Enlightenment, without heresy. A fancy story and no politics.\"","Mina's anger steadied her. \"Our class studies how fascism closed schools. You called that unclean history. Then you wanted the panel about democracy removed because elitism might offend your friends.\"","\"Liberalism and conservatism aren't your advertising departments,\" Mara added. \"A museum can examine an ideology without making every visitor subscribe to a doctrine.\"","Loren claimed he wanted focus on the art. His intent sat badly with his campaign of intrigue. The original's genre was a conversation: astronomy and genetics beside a classroom, a reminder that no single sub-discipline could explain a whole life. One facet celebrated creativity; another admitted the hindrance of poor eyesight. The artist's intellect had never exempted him from mistakes. His grammar was sometimes odd, his logic always open to challenge. Loren's replacement made the schoolmaster point at a throne.","\"You changed the concept,\" Mina said. \"You made an imitation of your own mindset.\"","Mara noticed Cassian's gaze settle in the middle distance. A uniformed carrier was crossing the garden below, pushing an empty crate. In a random gust, its paper cover lifted. The inside bore fresh scratches matching the canvas rack. Mara could correlate those marks with the narrow service door. The thief had not escaped with the art; he was returning to collect it.","\"We need him to oblige us by approaching the officers,\" Cassian said.","His humorous calm gave Mara an insight. She asked Mina to re-share the museum's accurate notice: the painting had been located, with police at the dome. It was a deliberately public warning, not a fabricated rescue story. Below, the carrier saw his phone, turned, and met an officer at the gate. He tried to elude her, found the other gate closed, and sat down on the crate.","Loren stared. \"You ruined months of work.\"","\"You could pour your energy into restoring the collection instead,\" Mara said. \"It doesn't create comprehension in the people who hear it.\"","His innate vanity had put him in a bind. The senior executive had mistaken novelty for innovativeness. He offered money if Mina would describe the stunt as a fantasy performance. She stubbornly refused. Her literacy course was being inspected tomorrow; he knew precisely how to hurt her. Cassian stepped beside her, but let her speak. Her dignity was not a bonus he could give away.","\"Traditionally,\" Mina said, \"the powerful call the poor vulgar when we ask what happened to the money. I won't repeat that tradition.\"","Loren's face looked malignant in the green emergency light, although the pallor was a physiological effect of the lamp. Mara kept that distinction clear in her own mind. The new evidence could cast doubt on his explanation; a frightening face established nothing. She let the officers take over without letting Loren violate the girl's space.","Downstairs, Sura found the canvas secure in its rack. Her mistrust of her own staff began to lift. The craze had exposed a paradoxical truth: a museum of questions had been afraid to ask questions about its sponsor. She would change its methodology for accepting gifts and stop the habit of promising to backload unpopular costs.","Cassian retrieved Mara's shoes. \"Will you dub this our most romantic evening?\"","\"There was a difficult choice between the rope and your excellent restraint.\"","\"I can provide both again.\"","She kissed him beside the rescued composite painting. Behind them, Mina gave a startled laugh. On the open notebook lay the artist's meditation: a small sketch, rescued from guesswork by patient awareness. Mara left it there. The night's best memento was the guide walking out with her head raised."],"questions":[],"wordCount":962,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0155\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：The Open Room</div><h1>The Roof Outside Their Comfort Zone</h1><p id=\"chapter-0155-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5700\" data-words=\"5700\">On</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2522\" data-words=\"2522\">dome</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7156\" data-words=\"7156\">roof</a>, Mara <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2411\" data-words=\"2411\">discovered</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8383\" data-words=\"8383\">that</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> executive&#x27;s <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2052\" data-words=\"2052\">cunning</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-2502\" data-words=\"2502\">did</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-5566\" data-words=\"5566\">not</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3057\" data-words=\"3057\">extend</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8538\" data-words=\"8538\">to</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8120\" data-words=\"8120\">suitable</a> footwear. <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3941\" data-words=\"3941,3907\">His</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6264\" data-words=\"6264\">polished</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7724\" data-words=\"7724\">soles</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7646\" data-words=\"7646\">slipped</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-5700\" data-words=\"5700\">on</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-9189\" data-words=\"9189\">wet</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1902\" data-words=\"1902\">copper</a>. The media had found an attractive genre: a duke, his beautiful wife, and a museum roof. Sura's agenda was less glamorous. She wanted the original returned to its proper axis, Mina's name cleared, and the literacy class allowed to open. Mara's goal was to accomplish all three before Cassian persuaded her to abandon public life for the bakery.","\"Your eyesight improves whenever cake appears,\" she told him.","\"An innate talent. But I'll exercise restraint until breakfast.\"","The first visitors met the imitation in the entrance hall. Sura had chosen to show its characteristic mistakes beside photographs of the real composite. This was no cunning bait for a new craze. A plain notice explained the current investigation, the confirmed recovery, and the unresolved insurance questions. Its grammar had been checked by Mina's students. The old director's reluctance to share control had become a practical lesson in democracy.","Loren's statement arrived through his lawyer. He called the plan an exercise in creativity that conservative trustees had misunderstood. His intention, he wrote, had been to pour his energy into saving the festival. The downside of his explanation was the message explicitly telling his carrier to single out Mina if anyone asked about the painting. Nobody needed guesswork to recognize that intent. The promised bonus also appeared in his contract.","Mina read the documents in silence. \"I want my job. I don't want my whole life to become this intrigue.\"","Mara understood the dilemma. Public vindication could oblige a person to keep performing pain. She asked the crowd not to re-share the girl's photograph. A correction could name the responsible office without turning its victim into a permanent memento. Cassian arranged a quiet door, and Mina chose to remain for the class. It was her consideration to weigh.","A trustee objected to displaying the false canvas. \"It is vulgar. It damages our heritage.\"","\"Its imitation is certainly poor,\" Sura said. \"But hiding how we were fooled would preserve our comfort zone, not the collection.\"","A different trustee thought the political panel should go. Its discussion of fascism made donors uneasy. Mina asked him to read the whole facet: a school shut by violence, followed by an argument about liberalism and conservatism in the rebuilding. The painting did not equate every ideology. Its conception of education required people to examine the consequences of a doctrine. Removing uncomfortable history would make a malignant habit of disregard seem like good manners.","\"Would you call questions about elitism heresy?\" Mina asked.","The trustee glanced toward Cassian, perhaps hoping his title would settle the matter. Cassian was studying a student's drawing of him as an enormous hat.","\"Excellent geometry. Clearly my portrait requires advanced maths,\" he said. \"The head underneath is less satisfactory.\"","Mara laughed, and the trustee lost his chance to borrow a duke's authority. This museum's enlightenment would have to depend on comprehension rather than a famous visitor's approval.","In the workshop, the canvas turned again. Its astronomy panel showed a child measuring a shadow. The archaeology scene opposite contained the counterpart of a broken bowl on a real display shelf. Beneath a painted pear, a recent annotation discussed genetics; the museum clearly distinguished that later addition from the original artist's work. The methodology mattered. A new interpretation could enrich a concept without being smuggled into an old object as fact.","One student asked whether an expert's intellect made his judgment automatic. Mara let her examine the false picture's repaired corner. A practised hand had made a clever patch, yet the skull faced the wrong way. An insight in restoration did not establish mastery of every sub-discipline. Sura had to correlate physical findings with records, not simply trust an impressive biography. The students found that logic more interesting than the fantasy of an infallible detective.","Cassian's little admirer offered him the hat drawing. \"It's a memento mori,\" she announced. \"You're old.\"","\"A devastating meditation,\" he said gravely.","\"You're twenty-nine,\" Mara reminded her.","\"Nearly thirty,\" the child answered, and departed with an adult's profound certainty.","The interruption dissolved the remaining mistrust in the room. Sura invited criticism of the festival's blueprint. The children wanted low worktables; their parents wanted clearer prices. A cleaner described the hindrance of storing crates across an exit. None needed to dub herself a genius to contribute. Their awareness of practical difficulties was an element the fancy launch had missed.","Mara had expected theory to bind them to an endless dispute. Instead, the visitors moved tables and opened a walkway. The museum's inefficiency became something they could repair together. It was paradoxical that a theft had made participation easier, but Sura refused to call the crime a blessing. The work was the citizens' achievement. Loren could not appropriate their success as proof of his innovativeness.","At noon, Mina's class began. Her first question concerned the picture's middle distance: why had the painter left an empty chair there? Two students imagined an absent teacher, another a place for a stranger. Mina allowed fancy to produce possibilities, then asked which details supported them. They could distinguish an interpretation from a claim about events. Creativity and discipline took turns instead of fighting for the same chair.","Mara left the skull sculpture in the garden. Someone had scrubbed its base, which had been unclean after the rain. Cassian waited beside it, holding breakfast in a paper bag. His mindset had survived the lost morning remarkably well.","\"A risky husband might resent being second to a painting,\" he said.","\"A wise husband would offer me the larger pastry.\"","He did. She pulled him beneath the pear tree and kissed the sugar from his mouth. Behind them, the Open Room turned toward the sun, no longer an excuse for certainty, but a place where a girl who had been falsely accused could ask the next question."],"questions":[],"wordCount":980,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0156\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：The Open Room</div><h1>The Empty Chair</h1><p
